Lemma C.20.

If
{\mathsf{S}}\not\leq\vcentcolon{\mathsf{S}}^{\prime}
there is no
\mathsf{v}
such that
\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\downarrow\mathsf{v}.

Proof.

By case analysis, we consider expression
expr
\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis
:

•

\mathtt{int}\not\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{nat}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{nat}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf succ}(-1)>0);

•

\mathtt{bool}\not\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{nat}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{true}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{nat}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf succ}(\mathsf{true})>0);

•

\mathtt{nat}\not\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{bool}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{bool}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=\neg 1;

•

\mathtt{int}\not\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{bool}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{bool}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=\neg(-1);

•

\mathtt{bool}\not\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{int}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathsf{true}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{int}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf inv}(\mathsf{true})>0).

In each case, the expression is undefined since the following expressions are undefined:
{\sf succ}(-1),{\sf succ}(\mathsf{true}),\neg 1,\neg(-1),{\sf inv}(\mathsf{true}).

∎

Lemma C.21.

If
{\mathsf{S}}\leq\vcentcolon{\mathsf{S}}^{\prime}
then
\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\downarrow\mathsf{true}
or
\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}S^{\prime}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\downarrow\mathsf{false}.

Proof.

By case analysis:

•

\mathtt{nat}\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{nat}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{nat}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf succ}(1)>0)\downarrow\mathsf{true};

•

\mathtt{int}\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{int}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}-1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{int}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf inv}(-1)>0)\downarrow\mathsf{true};

•

\mathtt{nat}\leq\vcentcolon\mathtt{int}:

\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}1},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{int}}\rrparenthesis$}}}=({\sf inv}(1)>0)\downarrow\mathsf{false}.

∎

Lemma C.22.

Let
\mathtt{pt}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime})\subseteq\{{\sf p}_{k}:1\leq k\leq m\}
and

{\mathsf{M}}={\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h_{k})

Then,

{\mathsf{M}}\longrightarrow^{*}{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}({\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h^{\prime}_{k})

where for all
1\leq k\leq m
:

h^{\prime}_{k}\equiv h_{k}\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{1}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{1}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\cdot\ldots\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{n}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{n}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))

when

\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}=\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{1}.{\sf p}_{k}?\ell_{1}({\mathsf{S}}_{1}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{2}.\ldots\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{n}.{\sf p}_{k}?\ell_{n}({\mathsf{S}}_{n}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{n+1}

where instead of
\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{i}
contexts there could also be empty contexts, and if
{\sf p}_{k}?\notin\mathtt{act}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})})
then
h^{\prime}_{k}\equiv h_{k}
.

Proof.

By induction on context
\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}
.
We detail only the case of inductive step.
Without loss of generality assume
\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}={\sf p}_{1}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}
,
and let
{\sf p}_{1}={\sf p}_{m+1}
.
Then,

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}=

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\

\displaystyle{\sf p}_{1}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\sf r}!\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\sf p}_{2}!\ell(\mathtt{bool}).{\sf p}_{m}?\ell(\mathtt{bool}).{\tt cyclic}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{1},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{1}\triangleleft h_{1}\penalty\ |\penalty\

\displaystyle\prod\limits_{2\leq k\leq m}({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\sf p}_{k-1}?\ell(\mathtt{bool}).{\sf p}_{k+1}!\ell(\mathtt{bool}).{\tt cyclic}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h_{k})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\

\displaystyle{\sf p}_{1}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{1},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{1}\triangleleft h^{\prime\prime}_{1}\penalty\ |\penalty\

\displaystyle\prod\limits_{2\leq k\leq m}({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}.{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h^{\prime\prime}_{k})

\displaystyle=:{\mathsf{M}}_{1}

where
h^{\prime\prime}_{1}=h_{1}\cdot({\sf r},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))
and
h^{\prime\prime}_{k}=h_{k}
, and where in
{\mathsf{M}}
first participant
{\sf p}_{1}
enqueues the message and then all participants (except
{\sf r}
) participate in the cycle. Applying induction hypothesis on
{\mathsf{M}}_{1}
we obtain

{\mathsf{M}}_{1}\longrightarrow^{*}{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}({\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h^{\prime}_{k})

where for all
1\leq k\leq m
:

h^{\prime}_{k}\equiv h^{\prime\prime}_{k}\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{1}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{1}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\cdot\ldots\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{n}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{n}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))

when

\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}=\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{1}.{\sf p}_{k}?\ell_{1}({\mathsf{S}}_{1}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{2}.\ldots\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{n}.{\sf p}_{k}?\ell_{n}({\mathsf{S}}_{n}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{n+1}

where instead of
\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p}_{k})}_{i}
contexts there could also be empty contexts, and if
{\sf p}_{k}?\notin\mathtt{act}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1})
then
h^{\prime}_{k}\equiv h^{\prime\prime}_{k}
.
Since
\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}={\sf p}_{1}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1}
and

h^{\prime}_{1}\equiv h_{1}\cdot({\sf r},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{1}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{1}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\cdot\ldots\cdot({\sf r},\ell_{n}(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{n}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))
, or
h^{\prime}_{1}\equiv h_{1}\cdot({\sf r},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))
in case
{\sf p}_{1}?\notin\mathtt{act}(\mathcal{A}^{({\sf p})}_{1})
, we may conclude the proof.

∎

Proposition C.23.

Let
{\mathbb{U}}
and
{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}
be session types such that
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)
and
\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})\subseteq\{{\sf p}_{k}:1\leq k\leq m\}
and
{\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}={\tt cyclic}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})
.
If

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}\left({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\varnothing\right)

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft h_{\sf p}\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}\left({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft P_{k}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h_{k}\right),

where
{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\longrightarrow^{*}{\sf r}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft h_{\sf p}
and

\prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}\left({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\varnothing\right)\longrightarrow^{*}\prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}\left({\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft P_{k}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft h_{k}\right)

then,
{\mathsf{M}}\longrightarrow^{*}{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
and
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}\longrightarrow^{*}\mathsf{error}.

Proof.

The proof is by induction on the derivation of

\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)
.
We extensively use notation
{\mathsf{U}}=\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)
and
{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}=\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)
.
The cases for the last rule applied are derived from Table
7
.

We first consider the cases with
\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{U}})\neq\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{V}}^{\prime})
.

\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{U}})\neq\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{V}}^{\prime})

\text{\scriptsize[{n-UV-out-act}]}:

{\mathsf{U}}={\sf p}!\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathsf{U}}_{1}
and
{\sf p}!\not\in\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{V}}^{\prime})
.

In this case, if
{\sf p}\in\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})
by definition of characteristic session type and characteristic process,
{\sf r}?\not\in{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)
. Thus,

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\sf p}!\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathbb{U}}_{1}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{1}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft({\sf p},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}\longrightarrow\mathsf{error}

(by
[
err-ophn
]
)

If
{\sf p}\notin\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})
we use
{\mathsf{M}}\equiv{\mathsf{M}}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\mathbf{0}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing
and derive the analogous proof as above.

\text{\scriptsize[{n-UV-inp-act}]}:

{\mathsf{U}}=\texternal_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}({\mathsf{S}}_{i}).{\mathsf{U}}_{i}
and
{\sf p}?\not\in\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{V}}^{\prime})
.

In this case, if
{\sf p}\in\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})

by definition of characteristic session type and characteristic process,
{\sf r}!\not\in{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)
. Thus,

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left(\texternal_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}({\mathsf{S}}_{i}).{\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{\sf p}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}\longrightarrow\mathsf{error}

(by
[
err-strv
]
)

If
{\sf p}\notin\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})
we use
{\mathsf{M}}\equiv{\mathsf{M}}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\mathbf{0}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing
and derive the analogous proof as above.

\text{\scriptsize[{n-UV-out-act-R}]}:

{\sf p}!\not\in\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{U}})
and

{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}=\tinternal_{i\in I}{\sf p}!\ell_{i}({\mathsf{S}}_{i}).{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}_{i}
.

In this case, by definition of characteristic process,
{\sf p}!\not\in{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)
.

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}(\tinternal_{i\in I}{\sf p}!\ell_{i}({\mathsf{S}}_{i}).{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}_{i},{\sf p},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}\longrightarrow\mathsf{error}

(by
[
err-strv
]
)

\text{\scriptsize[{n-UV-inp-act-R}]}:

{\sf p}?\not\in\mathtt{act}({\mathsf{U}})
and
{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}={\sf p}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}_{1}
.

In this case, by definition of characteristic process,
{\sf p}?\not\in{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)
.

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}({\sf p}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}_{1},{\sf p},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\sf r}!\ell\langle\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\rangle.P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft P\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft({\sf r},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}\longrightarrow\mathsf{error}

(by
[
err-ophn
]
)

In the following cases,
{\mathsf{U}}
type tree is rooted with an external choice.

{\mathsf{U}}=\texternal_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}({\mathsf{S}}_{i}).{\mathsf{U}}_{i}

In these cases, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i},\text{ where }

\displaystyle P_{i}\equiv

\displaystyle\mathsf{if}\penalty\ \text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{i}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\penalty\ \mathsf{then}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ \mathsf{else}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)

According to Table
7
, we distinguish four cases (not already considered), depending on the form of
{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}.

[
n-UV-inp
]
:

{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}={\sf p}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathsf{V}}_{1}^{\prime}
and

\forall i\in I:\ell_{i}\neq\ell\;\vee\;{\mathsf{S}}\not\leq\vcentcolon{\mathsf{S}}_{i}\;\vee\;{\mathsf{U}}_{i}\not\leqslant{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}_{1}
.

Now we have

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}\equiv

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\tt cyclic}({\sf p}?\ell({\mathsf{S}}).{\mathbb{V}}_{1}^{\prime},{\sf p},{\sf r})\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft{\sf r}!\ell\langle\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\rangle.P^{\prime}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle{\sf r}\triangleleft\sum_{i\in I}{\sf p}?\ell_{i}(x_{i}).P_{i}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft P^{\prime}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft({\sf r},\ell(\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}))\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\mathsf{M}}_{1}

We now distinguish three cases.

•

\forall i\in I:\ell_{i}\neq\ell
:
Session
{\mathsf{M}}_{1}
reduces to
\mathsf{error}
by
[
err-mism
]
.

•

\exists i\in I:\ell_{i}=\ell\;\wedge\;{\mathsf{S}}\not\leq\vcentcolon{\mathsf{S}}_{i}
:

{\mathsf{M}}_{1}\longrightarrow{\sf r}\triangleleft\mathsf{if}\penalty\ \text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{i}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\penalty\ \mathsf{then}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ \mathsf{else}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft P^{\prime}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

By Lemma
C.20
and
[
err-eval
]
, the session reduces to
\mathsf{error}
.

•

\exists i\in I:\ell_{i}=\ell\;\wedge\;{\mathsf{S}}\leq\vcentcolon{\mathsf{S}}_{i}\;\wedge\;{\mathsf{U}}_{i}\not\leqslant{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}_{1}
:

{\mathsf{M}}_{1}\longrightarrow{\sf r}\triangleleft\mathsf{if}\penalty\ \text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{expr$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\text{\rm{\color[rgb]{0,0,1}\lx@text@underline{val$\llparenthesis{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}}\rrparenthesis$}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathsf{S}}_{i}}\rrparenthesis$}}}\penalty\ \mathsf{then}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ \mathsf{else}\penalty\ {\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{i}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft P^{\prime}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}_{1}

By Lemma
C.21
, we further derive

{\mathcal{A}}

Proposition C.24.

Let
{\mathbb{T}}
and
{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}
be session types such that
{\mathbb{T}}\not\leqslant{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}
. Then, there are
{\mathbb{U}}
and
{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}
with
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SO}}}
and
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SI}}}
and
{\mathbb{U}}\not\leqslant{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}
such that:

{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}{\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\left({\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\varnothing\right)\longrightarrow^{*}\mathsf{error},

where
\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})\subseteq\{{\sf p}_{k}:1\leq k\leq m\}
and
{\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}={\tt cyclic}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})
.

Proof.

If
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right)
, there are
{\mathsf{U}}
and
{\mathsf{V}}
such that
{\mathsf{U}}\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SO}}}
and
{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SI}}}
and
{\mathsf{U}}\not\leqslant{\mathsf{V}}^{\prime}.
By Corollary
C.13
, there are
{\mathbb{U}}
and
{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}
such that
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SO}}}
and
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SI}}}
and
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right).
Now the proof follows by Proposition
C.23
.
∎

See
5.13

Proof.

Let
{\mathbb{T}}
and
{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}
be such that
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right).

Then, by Proposition
C.24
there are
{\mathbb{U}}
and
{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}
with
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SI}}}
and
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right)\in\llbracket\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\right)\rrbracket_{\text{\tiny\sf{SO}}}
and
\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{U}}}\right)\not\leqslant\operatorname{\mathcal{T}}\!\left({{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\right),

such that,

(239)

{\sf r}\triangleleft{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \mathcal{M}_{{\sf r},{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}\longrightarrow^{*}\mathsf{error},

where

\mathcal{M}_{{\sf r},{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}=\prod\limits_{1\leq k\leq m}{\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\left({\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}\right)\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf p}_{k}\triangleleft\varnothing\right)

and

\mathtt{pt}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime})\subseteq\{{\sf p}_{k}:1\leq k\leq m\}
and
{\mathbb{U}}_{{\sf p}_{k}}={\tt cyclic}({\mathbb{V}}^{\prime},{\sf p}_{k},{\sf r})
.

By Proposition
5.11
,
\vdash Q_{1}:{\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}
implies there is a live typing environment
\Gamma
such that

\Gamma\vdash{\sf r}\triangleleft Q_{1}\penalty\ |\penalty\ {\sf r}\triangleleft\varnothing\penalty\ |\penalty\ \mathcal{M}_{{\sf r},{\mathbb{V}}^{\prime}}

Since by Proposition
5.6

\vdash{\mathcal{P}}\!\left({\mathbb{U}}\right):{\mathbb{T}},

we conclude the proof by (
239
).
∎
